--- seg-5f1882f4fbe72ac43c0f51c6 [machine-unreviewed] ---
TERM OF OPENING OF THE 2nd VOLUME
--- seg-83a35e170f888f660920649f [needs-review] ---
On the tenth day of the month of March in the year one thousand nine hundred and ninety-seven,
*~ in the city of Três Corajosos, State of Minas Gerais, at the School of Sergeants of the Arms, I begin the investigation of this 2nd volume of the Military Police Inquiry, in which the publication titled "INCIDENT IN VARGINHA", by authors Vittorio Pacaccini and Maxs Portes, is indicted, in accordance with the Portaria n° 009-Aj G.2 of January 29, 1997, from the Honorable Commander of the School of Sergeants of the Arms, attaching the following pages; for which, this present term is drawn up.
--- seg-b45ea1747e9bc54784520df7 [machine-unreviewed] ---
I,   Sos              , VINICIUS PROBA          DOS SANTOS,   3° Sergeant,
      serving as Secretary who wrote and signed this.
--- seg-ed963bee98f10970204e6db3 [machine-unreviewed] ---

                                           SS           eS
                                                              scrivao

I observed a strange phenomenon in the sky: a series of lights with unusual movements, forming a kind of grid or pattern. At first, I thought it might be a drone or some kind of aircraft, but its behavior was unlike anything I've seen before. The lights seemed to be communicating or following a specific sequence. I watched for several minutes, and then they suddenly disappeared. There was no sound, and it happened so fast that I'm still in shock. I've never seen anything like this before, and I'm not sure what it could have been. Some people might say it was a UFO, but I can't be certain.

On the eleventh day of the month of March in the year one thousand nine hundred and ninety-seven, in the city of Três Coragdes, State of Minas Gerais, at the School of Sergeants of the Arms, where the responsible for this inquiry was located, the witness below named was present and questioned about Document No. 009 AjG.2, dated January 29, 1997, from FI No. 006, after which the following was declared: MAURICIO ANTONIO SANTOS, 44 years old, natural of Bom Despacho-MG, son of Mr. Anténio Pinto dos Santos and Mrs. Conceição Pinto, married, Lieutenant Colonel of the Military Police of the State of Minas Gerais, resident at Rua Fleming No. 370, Novo Horizonte neighborhood, city of Varginha-MG, Commander of the Military Police, after the commitment of the 24th Battalion...
--- seg-d1b45329c41b916fb1e38eb7 [needs-review] ---
~,             in truth, declared: when asked if he knows the content of the indicted book in this IPM,
                        whom he knows only by reputation, having heard talk of it, and who is now viewing the volume for the first
                       time, he responded
         and on this occasion in the presence of the responsible for IPM; when asked how he became aware of the subject matter treated in the book in question, he answered that on January 12th of this year he assumed command of the 24th Battalion of Military Police stationed in Varginha and
                       that probably on January 19th, in that location, there was a strong storm with
                     many "winds, ~8om falling walls and trees, house roofs, which caused numerous
                     occurrences with the Fire Department in offensive policing and which were supported
                       by the Battalion. He said that on the following day he was approached by Mr. Ubirajara,
                       who identified himself as a ufologist accompanied by another Mr. whose name he did not recall;
                       he clarified that on that occasion the visitors asked him if there had been any occurrences
                       involving "extraterrestrial beings," to which he responded that among the occurrences
                       recorded at the Battalion, none of them pertained to "terrestrials." He added that the
                       aforementioned ufologist asked him if it was possible that some occurrences might have
                       involved IPM.

===== PAGE 17 [page-e945bf5d042448f7c12b3e3f] =====
--- seg-b5becec8ba406bf3f47e125b [needs-review] ---
The death of Soldier PM Marco Eli Cherese, sensationalized by the media as having been caused by an extraterrestrial creature, was in fact due to a strange virus contracted from a creature. The ex-soldier had a cyst under his left armpit that he had planned to have surgically removed some time ago, and his death occurred as a result of a severe hospital infection post-surgery, as per the report presented to me and filed in these records (pp. 225-23+). The witness added that the media exploited the story because Cherese had worked in P2, and they asked if the ex-soldier had had contact with the extraterrestrial, to which the witness replied that he had no connection to the event and contradicted the claim made by the deceased's sister.
--- seg-7b27b813435961f046780dca [needs-review] ---
He was not officially involved in any extraterrestrial encounter; when asked about the circumstances of the alleged creature's appearance, he responded that there may have been a misunderstanding on the part of the girls mentioned in the book due to the "rainy, windy, and lightning situation in a poorly lit area, the vision of the citizen shown in the photographs, known by the nickname 'mudinho', who lived likely in the Andere garden, near the Three Points Street in Varginha."

The documents in these proceedings (Case No. 204) describe this person as likely having a mental deviation, as he would spend hours bent over, getting distracted by small objects, messing with garbage, and his physical and anatomical structure, including "horns and other small details," correspond to the description of the extraterrestrial creature in the book authored by ufologist Vitério Pacaccini and Mr. Maxs, published in the book "General Facts, the Battle that affected the Military Police Command of Minas Gerais and the military personnel involved, particularly regarding the image."

He responded that a circular note was issued by the Battalion to the local population, press, and anyone interested, stating that the institution had been publicly discredited and considered a lie, causing irreparable damage to its reputation, and leading to ridicule from colleagues and the public. He added that, as a result, there has been great embarrassment, especially among his peers, who persistently ask about the captured ET. He stated that the investigation ended at 4:45 PM on the same day it began at 2:40 PM, and after reading and finding it according to the facts, the sergeant signed the testimony.

Two ‘0 confrontations with alleged extraterrestrial beings (ETS) in the outskirts of the city were captured by the Army.

Adhemar José Gevard, editor of the UFO magazine, returned to the city for new encounters with ufologists Uhirajara Rodrigues and Vittorio Pacaccini, who are constantly in contact with witnesses and traveling to search for evidence about the case.

Claudir Covo, from São Paulo, will return to Varginha this weekend, “with a series of novelties about the case,” as he stated. He will also perform a second autopsy on the barnyard animal, whose initial examination sparked controversy.

Martins adds that the fact has characteristics of a highly sophisticated hoax. Ufologist Ubijara, for example, states that these cases occur worldwide, not just in Varginha, and in all places, there are discrepancies, he concluded.

Varginha is known as a major coffee production center in the world and has gained notoriety due to its association with UFOs."

--- seg-23e7925226455079a409609e [needs-review] ---
'.
                      concludes. The entire protagonists of these events are Botelho, Carlos Varginha,
                  creatures studied in a military base in Paraby, São Paulo (Unicamp), a university in Campinas.
                  A ufologist, who states that "we present data collected from military sources, apparently
                  related to the capture of intelligent beings." According to witness statements, collected data
                  include pneumonia, respiratory insufficiency, and septicemia, a condition that led to the death
                  of one individual, identified as a police officer.
                  The observed object, which fell from the sky, was reportedly equipped and dressed in a manner
                  consistent with military capture procedures.
                  One of the witnesses, Rodritee, claims to have seen a UFO and its capture by military personnel
                  during the previous week. The testimony suggests an extraterrestrial creature.
                  The document also presents the opinion of experts in the field, including Claudeir, a urologist
                  who participated in the investigation, along with other specialists from the Rio de Janeiro
                  Ufology Group (Ufoldgicos-RJ) and the Fluminense Studies Association (Associagado de Petit
                  Antonio e Marco in SP).
                  This report serves to shed light on the subject, which has been kept secret by the Brazilian

“iKalosa

Unidentified flying objects (UFOs) have been reported since ancient times, with numerous sightings and close encounters documented throughout history. On January 19, 199 JS, a remarkable event occurred that left a lasting impression on the witnesses. A group of people observed a large, bright object in the sky, which they described as a blue-green disc with lights flashing in a specific pattern.

The object moved swiftly, changing direction abruptly, and at one point, it seemed to hover in place. It was so close that the observers could see details like small windows or lights on its surface. After a few minutes, the UFO suddenly accelerated and disappeared at a high speed.

This particular sighting is just one example of many similar reports worldwide, where people from different backgrounds and cultures have described similar phenomena. Some of these encounters have been investigated by official bodies, but many remain unexplained, fueling speculation about extraterrestrial visitation or advanced aerial technologies.

The study of UFOs, or ‘unidentified aerial phenomena’ (UAP), continues to fascinate researchers, scientists, and the general public alike, as we continue to search for answers to these mysterious sky sightings.

It moved erratically, changing direction rapidly. The witnesses were unable to determine its origin or purpose. They reported the sighting to local authorities.

The object's path was observed from a distance, and it was noted to be moving at an extremely high speed. It was seen for approximately 10 seconds before disappearing. There were no known similar sightings in the area previously.

The witnesses' descriptions do not match any known aircraft or natural phenomena. The local authorities have classified the incident as an unidentified flying object (UFO) and are conducting further investigations.

UNCLASSIFIED

On 15th of the month, an unusual phenomenon was reported by several witnesses in the area. The description suggests a possible UFO encounter:

At approximately 10:00 AM, a group of individuals observed eight peculiar objects in the sky, which appeared to be moving in an organized manner. The objects were described as having a glowing, oval shape, and they seemed to be emitting a soft light.

One witness, who wished to remain anonymous, reported that the objects flew in a formation resembling a V-shape, moving at a speed faster than any known aircraft. They mentioned that the objects were not military aircraft or drones, as they did not display any visible signs of propulsion or control.

As the objects approached, they appeared to be descending towards a nearby field, but suddenly changed direction and flew away at a high speed. The witnesses agreed that the phenomenon was not a natural occurrence and speculated that it could be a UFO (Unidentified Flying Object).

The incident has been reported to local authorities, and a team of experts is investigating the matter. They are collecting statements from witnesses and analyzing any available visual evidence, including photographs and videos.

The UFO was described as having a distinct CBU shape and emitting a pulsating light. It is believed to have been part of a larger group, as multiple witnesses reported seeing them.

The authorities are treating the case with sensitivity, as UFO sightings have been on the rise in the region, raising speculation about potential extraterrestrial activity.

Further updates will be provided as the investigation progresses.
